=== PROMPT VARIANT ===
r1

=== PROMPT ===
You will be given a competitive programming problem.
You are given an array of integers nums and an integer k.
Return the maximum sum of a subarray of nums, such that the size of the subarray is divisible by k.
 
Example 1:

Input: nums = [1,2], k = 1
Output: 3
Explanation:
The subarray [1, 2] with sum 3 has length equal to 2 which is divisible by 1.

Example 2:

Input: nums = [-1,-2,-3,-4,-5], k = 4
Output: -10
Explanation:
The maximum sum subarray is [-1, -2, -3, -4] which has length equal to 4 which is divisible by 4.

Example 3:

Input: nums = [-5,1,2,-3,4], k = 2
Output: 4
Explanation:
The maximum sum subarray is [1, 2, -3, 4] which has length equal to 4 which is divisible by 2.

 
Constraints:

1 <= k <= nums.length <= 2 * 10^5
-10^9 <= nums[i] <= 10^9

Use the following starter code to write the final solution.
```python
class Solution:
    def maxSubarraySum(self, nums: List[int], k: int) -> int:
        
```

Reason in a commit-implement-recheck way.

A productive rhythm for this task usually looks like this:
- Isolate the decisive constraints, the target complexity, and at most one or two approaches that are still truly plausible.
- If two candidates remain, compare them only on the decisive constraint and commit quickly to the stronger one instead of keeping a wide search open.
- Before coding, keep only a tiny implementation ledger for the chosen plan: main invariant, key state or transition, and the one or two edge cases most likely to fail.
- Move into code as soon as that ledger is clear. After coding starts, keep reasoning attached to the current draft rather than reopening the high-level search.
- After the first complete draft, do one short implementation-check-adjust-check loop on the same draft: test the key edge case, patch locally if needed, and recheck.
- Reopen the main design only if a concrete counterexample breaks the core invariant. Otherwise prefer the local patch and keep closing.
- Once the current draft, invariant, and short recheck still agree, finalize the solution.

Output only the final Python code enclosed in a code block.

Need final code:

from typing import List

class Solution:
    def maxSubarraySum(self, nums: List[int], k: int) -> int:
        INF = 10**30
        min_pref = [INF] * k
        min_pref[0] = 0
        pref = 0
        ans = -INF
        for i, x in enumerate(nums, 1):
            pref += x
            r = i % k
            if min_pref[r] != INF:
                ans = max(ans, pref - min_pref[r])
            if pref < min_pref[r]:
                min_pref[r] = pref
        return ans

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def maxSubarraySum(self, nums: List[int], k: int) -> int:
        INF = 10**30
        min_pref = [INF] * k
        min_pref[0] = 0
        pref = 0
        ans = -INF

        for i, x in enumerate(nums, 1):
            pref += x
            r = i % k
            if min_pref[r] != INF:
                ans = max(ans, pref - min_pref[r])
            if pref < min_pref[r]:
                min_pref[r] = pref

        return ans
```

P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P? P?
</think>

=== ANSWER ===
```python
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def maxSubarraySum(self, nums: List[int], k: int) -> int:
        INF = 10**30
        min_pref = [INF] * k
        min_pref[0] = 0

        pref = 0
        ans = -INF

        for i, x in enumerate(nums, 1):
            pref += x
            r = i % k

            if min_pref[r] != INF:
                ans = max(ans, pref - min_pref[r])

            if pref < min_pref[r]:
                min_pref[r] = pref

        return ans
```
